43: 
44: The implementation resolves exact CAS bytes and typed source DTOs before projecting. Parent membership is checked by the compiled owner over the real leaves, rather than by a flag from S8. Source-bound v2 authorization checks the compiled ref, leaf ref and node; the signed frontier must name the same leaf source and its proposed selections must belong to that source. This admits scoped selection permission only: the new disposition disclaims Pareto, empirical, legal and publication authority.
45: 
46: Deployment trust enters through the typed app/container/service constructor path. Candidate context is parsed only as evidence refs; malformed `trust` input is a typed refusal. The S8 owner checks independent signatures, configured roles/case/scope/mandate, status, effective time and expiry. Current disposition reconstruction compares the persisted historical object with recomputed content and then computes the current response; stored green fields do not authorize emission. Historical v1 admission still resolves via its own typed schema.
47: 
48: Both outward job readers delegate to `_current_normative_job_record`. Missing or corrupted sidecars with a readable compiled source produce a fresh typed refusal preserving the source fronts; unreadable source becomes `not_established`, not empty. This is an honest fallback, and the return does not mint source or institutional authority. PA1-R01 concerns advancing to **new evidence**, not replaying the current evidence incorrectly.
49: 
50: The CAS adapter unwraps only the exact runtime `GuardedDependencyProxy` to its existing `FileSystemCAS`; it does not open a second store or accept arbitrary `_target` objects. The existing CAS itself continues enforcing ownership on bytes/manifests/signatures. This bypasses the proxy's timeout/circuit-breaker wrapper for S8 calls because the existing S8 owner accepts concrete CAS only; ownership is not implemented by that wrapper. No authority leak was established from this adapter. Supporting other backends remains the explicitly stated adapter boundary.
51:

12: production CG2 binding. That mechanism is already implemented and exercised in the stage-1
13: production-port tests.** In particular, the existing EFFECT production-port test persists its
14: source and bridge, then refuses `effect_binding_authority_not_established`. A decision to do
15: nothing merely because the full promotion cannot succeed would be wrong.
16: 
17: **This audit did not establish an existing candidate-bound source that the default N9
18: composition could consume today.** It established concrete upstream source failures and a
19: loss of source objects at the actual default boundary. It does **not** establish that a generic
20: source adapter cannot be engineered. Preserve the residual labels `producer_missing` and
21: `bridge_missing`; neither a new signature slot nor an empty default context would discharge
22: them. D2's no-production-edit decision is defensible as avoiding a second always-empty carrier,
23: not as a claim that the first-promotion engineering work is completed or outside PR1.

2: 
3: **Decision: this is a load-bearing sibling-consumer escape, not documentary evidence.**
4: The S3 law owner refuses the real law/knob correspondence, but the current L6-to-credal
5: projection unconditionally labels it `confirmed`. The actual CG0 atom consumer then emits
6: `admissibility=passed`. This belongs to S3's changed law-resolution handoff. Repair only that
7: projection and its deciding proof; do not reopen general S2/CG1 algorithms or weaken their
8: existing reference-status predicates.
9: 
10: ## Property, implementation, divergent case
11: 
12: `credal_reference.py`'s modality definition explicitly names
13: `L6_LEX_INTERVENTION_MAP` as **“JTCG law-to-knob admissibility.”**
14: `CredalReference.all_essential_confirmed` calls itself the CGF bind predicate.
15: `_iter_l6_edges` calls the real `resolve_law_bound_lever`, but tests only whether it throws.
16: A successfully constructed typed `blocked` resolution proceeds into `_confirmed_edge`, whose
17: single fixed completion is labelled `owner_signal_confirmed`.
18: 
19: This is authority promotion by exception absence. The decisive predicate is whether the
20: mapping is established; the implementation measures whether a resolver returned an object.
21: The divergent case is already the complete real source vocabulary: the three owner outputs
22: are `blocked`, `mapping_predicate_provenance=consumer_asserted`, and
23: `mapping_reason_code=law_mapping_correspondence_not_established`; every corresponding credal
24: edge is `confirmed`.

29:    `completion.value.knob_id`, places them in each matching atom's `edge_scope`, lifts the
30:    complete scope, and sets `admissibility="passed"` iff all scoped statuses are confirmed.
31: 2. `GroundingBindGate._safe_set` re-resolves that exact scope and requires every support status
32:    to be confirmed. Its `support_confirmed` check is an actual bind predicate.
33: 3. The law-token parser can also use the declared association for candidate interpretation.
34:    That candidate use is compatible with an uncertain edge; it does not require classifying
35:    an unresolved mapping as confirmed authority.

38: certificate or a promotion receipt, and does not assert that any complete production
39: promotion currently succeeds through this escape. Full K_ref generation remains subject to
40: the separately measured academic confidence-forwarding restriction.

94: the actual declared law/knob association while carrying the real resolver disposition and

101: as `status="admissible"`; P37 classification must remain visible. Current mappings have no
102: authority-grade positive source, so a fabricated positive mapping is not a completion
103: witness. An actual independently verified future mapping can use the existing confirmed
104: branch under its governed source epoch. Keep historical reference/receipt bytes under their
105: own epochs; record this handoff correction as part of S3's governed semantic change before
106: editing production code.

109: extraction and CG0 atom scope construction**, require the full real map denominator to remain
110: represented, and require each affected atom to retain its map edge and refuse confirmed
111: admissibility. Its removal probe restores unconditional confirmation while keeping provenance
112: fields and markers: the gate must go red. A second removal probe keeps `incomplete` but drops

The completed widening uses one private absence sentinel through the constructor, lazy wrapper, and reentry; only that sentinel omits the source key. 1: """Observe the unchanged actual DS17 example worker and retain its full dependencies."""
2: from __future__ import annotations
3: 
4: import hashlib
5: import json
6: import os
7: from pathlib import Path
8: import subprocess
9: import time
10: 
11: 
12: ROOT = Path.cwd()
13: SCRATCH = ROOT / ".tmp" / "gyphase5-ds17-dependency-audit"
14: SCRATCH.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
15: original_run = subprocess.run
16: workers = []
17: 
18: 
19: def observe_run(*args, **kwargs):
20:     result = original_run(*args, **kwargs)
21:     argv = args[0] if args else kwargs.get("args")
22:     if isinstance(argv, (list, tuple)) and any(
23:         str(part).endswith("governed_projection_validation_worker.py") for part in argv
24:     ):
25:         workers.append({
26:             "argv": list(argv),
27:             "cwd": str(kwargs.get("cwd")),
28:             "request": kwargs.get("input"),
29:             "returncode": result.returncode,
30:             "stdout": result.stdout,
31:             "stderr": result.stderr,
32:         })
33:     return result
34: 
35: 
36: started = time.monotonic()
37: subprocess.run = observe_run
38: try:
39:     from polisyos.runtime.http.openapi_contract import _confidence_ledger_risk_spend_example
40:     packet = _confidence_ledger_risk_spend_example()
41: finally:
42:     subprocess.run = original_run
43: 
44: (SCRATCH / "worker-observation.json").write_text(json.dumps({
45:     "invocation": "python -m gyphase5_ds17_dependency_audit",
46:     "cwd": str(ROOT), "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH"),
47:     "PYTHONPATH": os.environ.get("PYTHONPATH"),
48:     "elapsed_seconds": time.monotonic() - started,
49:     "workers": workers, "example": packet,
50: }, indent=2, default=str) + "\n")
51: if len(workers) != 1:
52:     raise RuntimeError("unexpected_worker_identity_set")
53: receipt = json.loads(workers[0]["stdout"])
54: bindings = receipt["dependency_bindings"]
55: from polisyos.runtime.http.services.governed_projection_dependencies import DependencyTracker
56: tracker = DependencyTracker(ROOT)
57: recomputed = {path: tracker.identity(ROOT / path) for path in sorted(bindings)}
58: delta = [{"path": path, "worker": bindings[path], "now": recomputed[path]}
59:          for path in sorted(bindings) if bindings[path] != recomputed[path]]
60: changed = subprocess.run(
61:     ["git", "diff", "--name-only", "3d572c146", "--", "."],
62:     cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True,
63: ).stdout.splitlines()
64: changed = [path.removeprefix("policy-engine/") for path in changed]
65: categories = {
66:     "openapi": [path for path in bindings if "openapi" in path.lower()],
67:     "lane_documents": [path for path in bindings if "gy-phase5" in path],
68:     "runtime_clients": [path for path in bindings if path.startswith("packages/runtime-api-client/")],
69:     "client_generator": [path for path in bindings if path == "tools/ops_runners/runtime/generate_runtime_client.py"],
70:     "slice_changed_dependencies": sorted(set(bindings).intersection(changed)),
71: }
72: out = {
73:     "worker_status": receipt["status"], "worker_issue_codes": receipt["issue_codes"],
74:     "dependency_aggregate_identity": receipt["dependency_aggregate_identity"],
75:     "complete_dependency_bindings": bindings,
76:     "independent_identity_recompute_delta": delta,
77:     "categories": {key: {path: bindings[path] for path in sorted(paths)} for key, paths in categories.items()},
78:     "actual_example": packet,
79: }
80: (SCRATCH / "dependency-analysis.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=2, default=str) + "\n")
81: print(json.dumps({
82:     "receipt_path": str(SCRATCH / "worker-observation.json"),
83:     "analysis_path": str(SCRATCH / "dependency-analysis.json"),
84:     "worker_status": out["worker_status"], "issues": out["worker_issue_codes"],
85:     "dependency_aggregate_identity": out["dependency_aggregate_identity"],
86:     "independent_identity_recompute_delta": delta,
87:     "categories": out["categories"],
88: }, indent=2))

1: # Independent Foundry facade delta review — 2026-09-08
2: 
3: No findings in this bounded D4 review. **Approve the Foundry facade exports, existing-owner imports and same-owner reentrancy correction for final integration.** D3f remains outside this review.
4: 
5: ## Specification and owner placement
6: 
7: `polisyos.foundry` exports `MethodRouteConstraint` and `method_accepts_input_contract` through its existing lazy map, type-checking imports and public export list. Each points to the existing selection owner; no wrapper, second class, nested manifest parser or second input-contract predicate is introduced. S3's new corresponding imports in `intervention_substrate.py` and the route-constraint annotation in `generation_cycle.py` use that facade. Existing unrelated imports and completed owner algorithms are outside this correction.
8: 
9: `test_route_constraint_facade_reuses_actual_input_contract_owner` resolves the actual facade exports and compares object identity with the existing advisor owner. It then calls that same predicate with the actual survival estimator's real input contract and a nonexistent contract, establishing both substantive acceptance and refusal. This is more than testing names in `__all__`.
10: 
11: ## Reentrant locking is required by an observed import path
12: 
13: The existing `_resolve_lazy_export` holds its owner lock across module import and attribute resolution, and acquires that lock before even reading its resolved-object cache. `d4-facade-python-stack.json` records an actual cold facade import, interrupted after 120 seconds with the complete Python stack. The chain begins at `MethodRouteConstraint`, imports the selection advisor and catalogue, reaches policy `frontier.py`, and reenters the same Foundry facade for its existing embedder exports. It stops at the second `_RESOLVE_LOCK` acquisition on the same thread. Thus the initial apparent slow import was a concrete nonreentrant-lock deadlock, not a reason to enlarge the timeout indefinitely.
14: 
15: Changing this single existing `threading.Lock` to `threading.RLock` preserves the serialized shared cache and exact owner lookup while allowing the observed same-thread nested export. It does not move imports outside synchronization or create a parallel resolver. No authority/receipt epoch or routing semantics change follows from that synchronization correction.
16: 
17: The finite regression runs in a separate process, warms real owner objects before timing synchronization, and makes the actual lazy resolver reenter itself through its import callback. The normal resolver must return the exact `advisor.MethodRouteConstraint` object. Replacing only the lock with a nonreentrant lock must hit the two-second synchronization timer. The child prints its success marker only after those behavioral assertions; the parent imposes a finite process budget. The altered lock/import callback cannot leak into other tests because the probe runs in its own process. This is a bounded reproduction of the observed mechanism, not a claim about every hypothetical cross-thread import topology.
18: 
19: ## Evidence readback and scope
20: 
21: Complete commands/outputs were read in the S3 evidence records:

DOCUMENT /Users/deniskopylov/polisyos/.worktrees/gy-phase5/policy-engine/docs/superpowers/journals/gy-phase5-evidence/shared/posture_formatter_ownership.py
1: """Exercise canonical-byte ownership through the actual formatter and DS11 check."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import hashlib
6: import json
7: import os
8: import subprocess
9: import sys
10: import time
11: from pathlib import Path
12: 
13: 
14: def run(command: list[str], cwd: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
15:     """Capture one real owner invocation, including its complete result."""
16:     started = time.monotonic()
17:     result = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603 — fixed local owner commands.
18:         command, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False
19:     )
20:     return {
21:         "argv": command,
22:         "cwd": str(cwd),
23:         "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH"),
24:         "PYTHONPATH": os.environ.get("PYTHONPATH"),
25:         "returncode": result.returncode,
26:         "elapsed_seconds": time.monotonic() - started,
27:         "stdout": result.stdout,
28:         "stderr": result.stderr,
29:     }
30: 
31: 
32: def main() -> int:
33:     """Remove the writer boundary and require the unchanged freshness check red."""
34:     root = Path.cwd()
35:     dashboard = root / "apps/runtime-dashboard"
36:     artifact = dashboard / "public/atlas/trust-claim-posture.v1.json"
37:     config = dashboard / ".prettierignore"
38:     writer_bytes = artifact.read_bytes()
39:     config_bytes = config.read_bytes()
40:     entry = b"/public/atlas/trust-claim-posture.v1.json\n"
41:     if config_bytes.count(entry) != 1:
42:         raise ValueError("ownership_entry_ambiguous")
43:     formatter = [
44:         "corepack", "pnpm", "exec", "prettier", "--write", "--ignore-unknown",
45:         "public/atlas/trust-claim-posture.v1.json",
46:     ]
47:     checker = [
48:         sys.executable, "-m", "tools.quality.validation.check_trust_claim_posture",
49:         "--repo-root", ".", "--check",
50:     ]
51:     records: dict[str, object] = {}
52:     try:
53:         records["with_owner_boundary"] = run(formatter, dashboard)
54:         records["boundary_preserves_writer_bytes"] = artifact.read_bytes() == writer_bytes
55:         config.write_bytes(config_bytes.replace(entry, b""))
56:         records["without_owner_boundary"] = run(formatter, dashboard)
57:         formatted = artifact.read_bytes()
58:         records["removal_changes_bytes"] = formatted != writer_bytes
59:         records["removal_preserves_all_parsed_values"] = (
60:             json.loads(formatted) == json.loads(writer_bytes)
61:         )
62:         records["real_check_after_removal"] = run(checker, root)
63:     finally:
64:         config.write_bytes(config_bytes)
65:         artifact.write_bytes(writer_bytes)
66:     records["writer_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(writer_bytes).hexdigest()
67:     records["restored_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
68:     records["artifact_restored_exactly"] = artifact.read_bytes() == writer_bytes
69:     records["configuration_restored_exactly"] = config.read_bytes() == config_bytes
70:     sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(records, indent=2) + "\n")
71:     if not all(records[key] is True for key in (
72:         "boundary_preserves_writer_bytes", "removal_changes_bytes",
73:         "removal_preserves_all_parsed_values", "artifact_restored_exactly",
74:         "configuration_restored_exactly",
75:     )):
76:         raise ValueError("formatter_ownership_property_not_established")
77:     if any(records[key]["returncode"] != 0 for key in (  # type: ignore[index]
78:         "with_owner_boundary", "without_owner_boundary"
79:     )):
80:         raise ValueError("formatter_command_failed")
81:     checked = records["real_check_after_removal"]
82:     if checked["returncode"] != 1 or (  # type: ignore[index]
83:         "ValueError: DS11-GENERATED-DRIFT" not in checked["stderr"]  # type: ignore[index]
84:     ):
85:         raise ValueError("unchanged_owner_did_not_refuse_formatted_bytes")
86:     return 0
87: 
88: 
89: if __name__ == "__main__":
90:     raise SystemExit(main())

DOCUMENT /Users/deniskopylov/polisyos/.worktrees/gy-phase5/policy-engine/docs/superpowers/journals/gy-phase5-evidence/shared/posture_report_corruption.py
1: """Require the normal DS11 owner to refuse a corrupted content binding."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import hashlib
6: import json
7: import os
8: import subprocess
9: import sys
10: import time
11: from pathlib import Path
12: 
13: 
14: def main() -> int:
15:     """Mutate one digest, execute the actual check, and restore writer bytes."""
16:     path = Path("apps/runtime-dashboard/public/atlas/trust-claim-posture.v1.json")
17:     original = path.read_bytes()
18:     before = json.loads(original)
19:     original_digest = before["payload_digest"]
20:     false_digest = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
21:     if original_digest == false_digest:
22:         raise ValueError("writer_digest_already_zero")
23:     marker = json.dumps(original_digest).encode()
24:     if original.count(marker) != 1:
25:         raise ValueError("mutation_identity_ambiguous")
26:     corrupted = original.replace(marker, json.dumps(false_digest).encode())
27:     after = json.loads(corrupted)
28:     after["payload_digest"] = original_digest
29:     if after != before:
30:         raise ValueError("unexpected_additional_mutation")
31:     command = [
32:         sys.executable,
33:         "-m",
34:         "tools.quality.validation.check_trust_claim_posture",
35:         "--repo-root",
36:         ".",
37:         "--check",
38:     ]
39:     started = time.monotonic()
40:     try:
41:         path.write_bytes(corrupted)
42:         result = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603 — fixed owner command, no user input.
43:             command, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False
44:         )
45:     finally:
46:         path.write_bytes(original)
47:     restored = path.read_bytes()
48:     evidence = {
49:         "artifact": str(path),
50:         "mutation_path": "/payload_digest",
51:         "original_value": original_digest,
52:         "mutated_value": false_digest,
53:         "all_other_parsed_values_equal": after == before,
54:         "cwd": str(Path.cwd()),
55:         "argv": command,
56:         "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH"),
57:         "PYTHONPATH": os.environ.get("PYTHONPATH"),
58:         "returncode": result.returncode,
59:         "elapsed_seconds": time.monotonic() - started,
60:         "stdout": result.stdout,
61:         "stderr": result.stderr,
62:         "original_sha256": hashlib.sha256(original).hexdigest(),
63:         "restored_sha256": hashlib.sha256(restored).hexdigest(),
64:         "byte_identical_restoration": original == restored,
65:     }
66:     sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(evidence, indent=2) + "\n")
67:     if original != restored:
68:         raise ValueError("restoration_not_byte_identical")
69:     if result.returncode != 1 or "ValueError: DS11-GENERATED-DRIFT" not in result.stderr:
70:         raise ValueError("real_owner_did_not_refuse_corrupted_binding")
71:     return 0
72: 
73: 
74: if __name__ == "__main__":
75:     raise SystemExit(main())

DOCUMENT /Users/deniskopylov/polisyos/.worktrees/gy-phase5/policy-engine/docs/superpowers/journals/gy-phase5-evidence/shared/probe_framework_python.py
1: """Verify copied framework Python without inherited dynamic-loader settings."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import json
6: import os
7: import subprocess
8: import sys
9: import tempfile
10: import venv
11: from pathlib import Path
12: 
13: 
14: def main() -> None:
15:     """Execute both direct and system-shell children from a real copied venv."""
16:     environment = {
17:         key: value for key, value in os.environ.items() if not key.startswith("DYLD_")
18:     }
19:     records = []
20:     with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="gyphase5-framework-copy-", dir=".tmp") as scratch:
21:         private = Path(scratch).resolve() / "environment"
22:         venv.EnvBuilder(with_pip=False).create(private)
23:         python = str(private / "bin/python3")
24:         expression = (
25:             "import json,sys; print(json.dumps({'version':sys.version,'prefix':sys.prefix}))"
26:         )
27:         commands = [
28:             [python, "-c", expression],
29:             ["/bin/bash", "-c", 'exec "$1" -c "$2"', "probe", python, expression],
30:         ]
31:         for command in commands:
32:             child = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603 - measured local copied interpreter.
33:                 command, env=environment, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False
34:             )
35:             records.append({
36:                 "argv": command,
37:                 "returncode": child.returncode,
38:                 "stdout": child.stdout,
39:                 "stderr": child.stderr,
40:             })
41:         sys.stdout.write(json.dumps({
42:             "outer_executable": sys.executable,
43:             "outer_version": sys.version,
44:             "DYLD_environment_keys": [key for key in environment if key.startswith("DYLD_")],
45:             "records": records,
46:         }, indent=2) + "\n")
47:         for record in records:
48:             if record["returncode"] != 0:
49:                 raise RuntimeError("copied_framework_child_failed")
50:             result = json.loads(record["stdout"])
51:             if result["version"] != sys.version or result["prefix"] != str(private):
52:                 raise RuntimeError("copied_framework_child_escaped_private_prefix")
53: 
54: 
55: if __name__ == "__main__":
56:     main()

DOCUMENT /Users/deniskopylov/polisyos/.worktrees/gy-phase5/policy-engine/docs/superpowers/journals/gy-phase5-evidence/shared/probe_private_python.py
1: """Exercise the guardrail's exact copied-interpreter station prerequisite."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import json
6: import os
7: import subprocess
8: import sys
9: import sysconfig
10: import tempfile
11: import venv
12: from pathlib import Path
13: 
14: 
15: def main() -> None:
16:     """Retain the original loader failure and verify a library-path-only repair."""
17:     library = Path(sysconfig.get_config_var("LIBDIR"))
18:     dylib = library / sysconfig.get_config_var("LDLIBRARY")
19:     if not dylib.is_file():
20:         raise RuntimeError("measured_base_python_library_unavailable")
21:     records = []
22:     with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="gyphase5-python-copy-", dir=".tmp") as scratch:
23:         private = Path(scratch).resolve() / "environment"
24:         venv.EnvBuilder(with_pip=False).create(private)
25:         command = [
26:             str(private / "bin/python3"),
27:             "-c",
28:             "import json,sys; print(json.dumps({'version':sys.version,"
29:             "'base_prefix':sys.base_prefix,'executable':sys.executable}))",
30:         ]
31:         for mode in ("original", "base_library_path"):
32:             environment = os.environ.copy()
33:             environment.pop("DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH", None)
34:             if mode == "base_library_path":
35:                 environment["DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH"] = str(library)
36:             child = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603 - actual local copied interpreter.
37:                 command, env=environment, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False
38:             )
39:             records.append({
40:                 "mode": mode,
41:                 "argv": command,
42:                 "DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH": environment.get("DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH"),
43:                 "returncode": child.returncode,
44:                 "stdout": child.stdout,
45:                 "stderr": child.stderr,
46:             })
47:         sys.stdout.write(json.dumps({
48:             "library": str(dylib),
49:             "outer_version": sys.version,
50:             "records": records,
51:         }, indent=2) + "\n")
52:         if records[0]["returncode"] == 0 or records[1]["returncode"] != 0:
53:             raise RuntimeError("copied_interpreter_station_property_not_established")
54:         fixed = json.loads(records[1]["stdout"])
55:         if fixed["version"] != sys.version:
56:             raise RuntimeError("copied_interpreter_version_changed")
57: 
58: 
59: if __name__ == "__main__":
60:     main()
